# Known functions: the tuning screen

The front end's tuning shop: the screen where the player buys and fits
parts on the car shown on the stand, by category (the screen's +9656: 0
power, 1 power train, 2 body, 3 tire, 4 aero, 5 exterior, 6 interior,
with 4, 5, 1, 3, 10, 8 and 4 items, the item the byte +9660), then a
level, a type and an option (bytes +9661 to +9664), with the wheels'
brand and model lists, the vinyl and sticker editors (movies 13 and 14)
and the paint. Its update `sub_821E6C50` (reached through a vtable; with
the recompiler's pieces `sub_821E6CD4` and `sub_821E6F90`) runs a state
machine on +9652 (3 to 58), and for each state a handler that only
prints a debug menu through the empty `sub_82106540` (`audio.md`; the
print compiled out: "TUNING SHOP MAIN", "LEVEL_SELECT", "CONF BUY" and
the others are its format strings), then a command queue at `[+10036]`
(its +12 the current command, 0 to 64): each command's runner
(`TuningScreen::CommandN` here, and the page set-ups `PlayIntro`,
`MoveCategory`, `OpenItems`, `ShowLevels`, `ShowTypes`, `ShowOptions`,
`ShowDialog`, `MarkPick`) runs once when it starts, then `UiMovie::Run`
until its movie (2; 13 for states 23 to 37, 14 for 38 to 52) stops,
and the queue steps (+16 the next, -1 none). Every update then runs
`TuningScreen::UpdateItems`, `DrawPowerGraph` (`power-graph.md`),
`RefreshMenu`, `UpdateListDots`, `UpdateListCounter` and `UpdateStand`,
and while +9732 is set `StoreToGarageCar`, `SetCarStats` and
`RestoreStand`. While +9708 is 1 the update runs the refresh
`sub_821E3AD0` (3,838 lines: the screen's node set-up by state, which
calls `PickCameraPreset`, `PreviewItem`, `GetPrice`, `GetCurrentChoice`,
`GetChoiceFlag`, `AeroPartValue`, `EquipChoice` and the node helpers
here). The part tables (price, current choice and flags, by category
and item) are in the screen's +33900 to +36800; the choices go into the
selected garage car (`../known-data.md`'s "garage car") through
`StoreToGarageCar` and `EquipChoice`, and onto the stand's car through
`GarageStage::` setters (`stand-models.md`) by `PreviewItem`,
`RestoreStand`, `PreviewWheels`, `ApplyDecals`, `ApplyAreaDecals` and
`ApplyDecalWord`. Names are ours. The fields worked out are in
`../known-data.md` ("tuning screen", "garage car").

Mapped here (2026-11-07, the B7 loop's mapping agent, from the leads
"the tuning screen's update" and "the tuning screen's handlers that pick
the stand car's parts"): the update's subtree below the update and its
refresh, with `TuningScreen::PreviewWheels` (`sub_821E0E00`), and the
stand screens' functions it reaches (`sub_821D9F10`, `sub_821D99A0`,
`sub_821D9B60`, `sub_822019D0`, `sub_821D6100`, `sub_821E0FB0`); 60
ready groups among them. Left as leads (`../loops/leads.md`): the update
with its two pieces and the state handlers and command runners that
never ran in play (61 of them); the screen's other vtable slots (its input
pages); `GarageStage::SetColor`'s pieces and the stand's other setters
met here. The power curve's set-up `sub_821E7368` stayed in its lead (since 2026-11-09 `TuningMachine::Start` in [`tuning-machine.md`](tuning-machine.md), with the screen's machine).
The refresh `sub_821E3AD0` with its callees was mapped here later
(2026-11-11, the B7 loop's mapping agent, from its own lead): see "The
refresh" below and the last eight rows.
The update `sub_821E6C50` with its two pieces and its 61 callees that
never ran in play was mapped here last (2026-11-11, the B7 loop's
mapping agent, from its own lead): see "The update" below and the last
64 rows.

Shared, with rows elsewhere: `UiMovie::` and `UiMovies::Find`
(`ui-movies-game.md`, `ui-movies.md`: among them `sub_8211C740`,
`sub_8211D930` and `sub_8211CAA0`, which `RefreshMovie13` and others
call, native since 2026-11-07); `GarageStage::Draw` (`sub_8217FE60`,
which `UpdateStand` calls, native since 2026-11-07), `Command`, `IsBusy`,
`SetLightKit` (`front-end-car-draw.md`); the stand's record setters
(`GarageStage::SetPart`, `SetInteriorClass`, `SetInteriorWord`,
`SetInteriorLevel`, `SetInteriorPair`, `ClearChoice`, `SetChoice`,
`SetAero`, `SetAero2`, `SetDoorKind`, `SetTireGrade`, `ClearLightKit`,
`SetDecalA`, `SetDecalB`, `SetWheelWord`, `ReloadDecals`) with
`TuningScreen::SetTireGrade`, `StepSecondWheels` and `PreviewWheel`
(`stand-models.md`); `GarageStage::FitCar`, `SetCar` and
`TuningScreen::SetUpCarData` (`car-makers.md`); `TuningScreen::DrawPowerGraph`
(`power-graph.md`); `PowerCurve::FromEngine` and `ShiftPeak`
(`vehicle.md`); `Car::Init`, `Car::Refresh`, `Params::GetCarParts`,
`AeroParts::Get77`, `Get82` (`race-setup.md`, `car-specs.md`);
`SaveData::Of` (`vehicle-accessors.md`); `Strings::Format`
(`strings-and-font.md`); `memcpy`, `memset` (`c-runtime.md`); the empty
`sub_82106540` (`audio.md`).

Notes for the migration: `TuningScreen::StoreToGarageCar`
(`sub_821DCC28`) works below the stack pointer with no frame, reads
stack words it never wrote for some sticker values and returns a
division's leftover in r3 (copied as the original leaves them: "B7
changes no game behavior", `../loops/b7-loop.md`'s "Precedent first");
the debug handlers' calls to the empty `sub_82106540` pass varargs
(native callers can leave them out, as other native callers of it do).
`GarageStage::SetColor`'s wheel colors (its pieces 3 to 5) write the
scene model's +64 to +111, the paint's offsets, while
`SceneModel::ApplyWheelPaint`'s row reads +112 to +207: one of the two
to check (neither piece ran in play).

The refresh (`TuningScreen::Refresh`, `sub_821E3AD0`, mapped
2026-11-11): one function of 3,838 lines (b7_groups' count;
`generated/default/itc_recomp.50.cpp` lines 8236 to 15048), seen in
play, called by the update `sub_821E6C50` (a lead) while +9708 is 1 and
by `TuningMachine::Start` `sub_821E7368`
([`tuning-machine.md`](tuning-machine.md)), which it alone blocks:
`Start` is ready once the refresh is ours. Every callee seen in play is
already ours (the UI movie calls in `ui-movies-game.md` and
`ui-movies.md`, the helpers in this file, `GarageStage::SetLightsOn`,
`Command`, `SetLightKit`, `SetChoice`, `ClearChoice`,
`Params::GetCarParts`, `AeroParts::Get77`, `Get82`, `memcpy`); its
callees not seen in play are the last rows here. Its migration is to be
split (b7_groups: over 1,500 lines), by the blocks below (line numbers
in that file, labels `loc_821E....`). m = movie 2 throughout
(`UiMovies::Find` on `0x826C5CCC`, key 2), s = the screen, holder =
`[[0x826D83E0]]` (the stand's holder, as `GarageStage::` setters take).

Found in its migration's part 1 (2026-11-11): the header runs to 8836
(the switch); its `SetText`(m, 18, 8) sets r10 to the frame's +80 (the
`stfiwx` address of the weight's conversion, passed as the original sets

`SetCarStats`', child 12's double, `SetLightsOn`'s light value (flag 0)
and state 4's `AnimationTime`s, unless a guest call after them set it

reported by its plain C++; the light value overwrites them except with
the headlights' flag 1).

The update (`TuningScreen::Update`, `sub_821E6C50`, mapped 2026-11-11):
one function the recompiler cut into three (b7_groups: 1,601 lines, one
ready group, "split the migration"), all seen in play, reached only
through a vtable (the tuning machine's state 5, message 0,
[`tuning-machine.md`](tuning-machine.md)). r3 = the screen s, r4 = the
mode's dispatcher (kept in r28; passed on only to commands 30, 52 and
63). Its pieces repeat its tail, so the native update is one function;
line numbers are in `generated/default/itc_recomp.81.cpp` (the update
itself, 8380 to 9761) unless said:

1. The header (8380 to 8452): a 144-byte frame (`__savegprlr_26`); the
   empty `sub_82106540`(490, 280, 0, "TUNING SHOP" `0x8200ADA4`);
   while +9708 is 1, the refresh `sub_821E3AD0` (above) and +9708 =
   (its r3 is 0).
2. The state switch (8453 to 8879): state +9652 - 3 (unsigned, 0 to 55,
   else none) through the 8-bit table at `0x8200AA00`, each case its
   handler with r3 = s, then on to the tail: 3 `DebugMainMenu` (through
   the piece `sub_821E6CD4`: `itc_recomp.13.cpp` 8731 to 9621, the
   handler then the whole tail again), 4 `DebugCategoryMenu`, 5
   `DebugLevelSelect`, 6 `DebugTypeSelect`, 7 `DebugColorSelect`, 8
   `DebugOptionSelect`, 9 `DebugConfirmBuy`, 10 `DebugWarnMoney`, 11
   `DebugConfirmEquip`, 12 `DebugReflectSet`, 13 `DebugAnnulSet`, 14
   `DebugRemoveConfirm`, 15 to 17 the wheels' `DebugWheelPartSelect`,
   `DebugWheelKindSelect`, `DebugWheelInchSelect`, 18
   `DebugBodyColor`, 19 to 22 `DebugNeonPart`, `DebugNeonKind`,
   `DebugNeonEdit`, `DebugLedEdit`; 23 to 37 and 38 to 52 the same
   fifteen (vinyl, then sticker): `DebugDecalTopMenu`,

Every callee seen in play is already ours (the rows above and
`ui-movies-game.md`'s `Find` and `Run`, `power-graph.md`'s
`DrawPowerGraph`, `audio.md`'s empty `sub_82106540`). The 61 never seen
are the last 61 rows. A split for the migration: first the update
itself (blocks 1 to 6, about 1,600 lines in three pieces but a short
function in C++: two jump tables and the queue's step), calling the 61
never-seen callees through the guest, as the refresh calls its own
(`sub_821F7A98`); then the never-seen callees in groups: the 23
handlers (print only; the seen handlers' native versions write the
frame words and string tables they leave, a precedent to follow), the
vinyl editor's commands (movie 13: 17, 19, 20, 21, 26, 27), the sticker
editor's (movie 14: 32 to 53 and `Command32Done`) and movie 2's (5, 10
to 14, 54 to 58). The sticker editor's commands call helpers from the
sticker editor's lead (`../loops/leads.md`: `sub_821F7518`,
`sub_821F7F20`, `sub_821F8070`, `sub_821F8160`, `sub_821F8A50`,
`sub_821F8D68`, `sub_821FA408`, `sub_821FA508`, `sub_821FBB60`,
`sub_821F73F8`, with `sub_821FA460` added there): those groups wait on
that lead. The vinyl editor's `Command21` waited on `sub_821FD518` and
`sub_82207F38`, native since 2026-11-13 (`tuning-pages.md`).
Done that way (2026-11-11): the update, then the 23 handlers, movie 2's
5, 11, 12 and 54 to 58, and 17, 19, 20, 26, 27, 42, 52, 53 and
`Command32Done` (40 of the 61, Native with no adapters); left
recompiled, called through the guest: `Command21` and the sticker
editor's 32 to 41 and 43 to 51 (the leads above; 46, 48 and 50 one
function), and 10, 13 and 14, whose paint and light kit bar setters
(`tuning-pages.md`, never seen in play) are still recompiled. The
sticker editor's 32 to 41 and 43 to 51 followed on 2026-11-13, once its
helpers were ours (`sticker-editor.md`'s second migration:
`src/tuning_pages/sticker_commands.cpp`, called by the update's plain
C++ with `--native_tuning_pages`, no adapters); none reaches
`ApplyAreaDecals`' `sub_8217B3D8` or `sub_821793D0` (those are under
`UpdateStand`), so none was left recompiled. Their shapes: an opening
command per page resets the sticker menu m (+16 its last row, +20 the
wrap, m+0 the page's field) and, with +9720 (back from a deeper page),
plays the way back by the queue's previous state q+8, else the page's
entry animation; a cursor command (33, 37, 39, 41, 44) plays from m+4 to
m+0 and steps the queue; 46 (48, 50) only places D's node and its texts.

Found in its migration (2026-11-11): the movie for the run is chosen
from the state read again after the debug handler (23 to 37 13, 38 to 52
14, 53 to 60 2, 61 by the vinyl, else 2), and the queue's step after a
stopped run reads `[+10036]` again (a runner may have replaced the
queue). r30 at `DrawPowerGraph` (which its adapter saves in its frame) is
the run's "stopped" flag, or the queue for no command (-1) and commands
above 64; r31 is all 64 bits of r3. Nothing of the update itself is in
registers its callers read: it sets no return value (r3 `UpdateStand`'s
or `RestoreStand`'s). In a harness, `UiMovie::Run` draws the movie
(`UiMovie::Update`), which the tuning screen's text world can't: its
update is stood in for, as the garage screen's harness does.

Movie 2's never-seen commands with every callee ours (5, 11, 12 and 54
to 58) were made native on 2026-11-11 (`src/tuning_screen/commands.cpp`,
called straight from the update's plain C++, no adapters; 10, 13 and 14
still go through the guest). Each follows the seen runners' pattern
(`Command7`, `Command9`): the +9716 set-up (+9720 snapping the animation
to its end and stepping the queue), a node placed through the doubleword
at frame + 80, the page's named animation restarted and looped, the
queue's step with +9724. `Command57` alone tests animation times
(`UiMovie::NamedAnimationTime`, f1 left set) and names rows with
`SetNodeText`. In a harness, a looping-list slot naming an animation
needs its node and animation words (+12, +16) when the function also
finishes or plays that named animation: `FinishNamed` reads through
them (a slot with only its name and time crashed the original on 0xC).

The editors' never-seen commands with every callee ours (17, 19, 20, 26,
27, 42, 52, 53 and `Command32Done`) followed the same day, in the same
file: the vinyl editor's (movie 13) take the +9716 set-up without
+9724 (+9720 steps the queue instead of snapping), `Command26` and
`Command27` are `Command25` with another animation and help line;
`Command42` slides the sticker list's position +30756 one step toward
its target (the word at +33480 + 4 × position) and steps the queue only
once it's there; `Command52` is `Command30`'s dialog for the states 50
to 52, then shows movie 14's node (1, 1) whatever the state;
`Command32Done` colors its two nodes only with [+33508] set. In the
update's harness group, `Command42`'s words are laid from a generator of
their own: drawing them from the cases' generator moved the later cases'
worlds, and one (case 142, command 26, state 5) then read the wild guest
address 0x70081650 over and over in the original, a fragility of that
group's world not looked into further. In the all-harness race
(2026-11-11), its case 11 (state 14, the stand put back) read guest
0x700D0000, the harness thread's stack top, over and over: the restore
path's `StoreToGarageCar` wrote the screen's random engine stage
(+34304 + 4 × the part set) into the garage car, `SetCarStats`'
`Car::Refresh` copied the spec's engine record at that stage
(unchecked), and its random torque curve count (+1280) ran
`Vehicle::SetupFromCar`'s copy off the memory, as in the refresh's
state 5 group; the world only differs by its blocks' addresses between
runs. The group now lays the engine stages 0 to 9 over the world's
engine records and the tire types 0 to 3 for the restore path, from a
generator of their own (seeded by the case).

Status:

- **Native**: plain C++ in `itc_recomp/src/` that doesn't use the
  PowerPC context (`ctx`): normal arguments and host structs (it may
  still read and write guest memory through `src/guest.h` while
  recompiled code shares the data). The game reaches it without `ctx`,
  from native or overridden callers, and the recompiled original is no
  longer called (except behind a fallback flag or to check against it).
  No `REX_FUNC` override of it exists, not even an adapter kept only for
  a harness (that's Overridden until the adapter goes).
  The end state for every function.
- **Overridden**: an `extern "C" REX_FUNC(sub_XXXXXXXX)` override in
  `itc_recomp/src/` (or a macro defining one) does the work instead of
  the original: a thin adapter in front of native code, or a body that
  still uses `ctx` (calling guest code, reading registers). It may call
  the original when its flag is off, or to check itself against it.
- **Wrapped**: an override that does something of its own (skipping
  render-only passes, logging, timing) and still calls the original
  (`__imp__sub_XXXXXXXX`) for the work.
- **Dropped**: its work isn't needed on PC (hardware set-up, kernel calls
  the SDK already stubbed), so it no longer runs: an override in
  `itc_recomp/src/` replaces it with nothing (or a fixed return value), or
  its callers, made native, leave the call out (no override then; the row
  says which caller). Logs, a harness or the renderer's frame dumps have
  shown that nothing reads what it did. The original is no longer called,
  except behind a fallback flag while the drop is being checked. A
  function the game never calls in the first place isn't Dropped: its
  status stays blank and its row says it's never called.
- **Merged**: its work is done inside a native caller's plain C++ (a
  few lines only that caller needs), so it has no function or override of
  its own in `itc_recomp/src/`. The row names the caller(s) and the
  harness that covers the work. The original is no longer called, except
  behind the caller's fallback flag.
- **Patched**: the generated code was edited in place (search for
  `PATCH:`).
